        local scf = tcf * CFrame.new(0, -10, 0)
        car:PivotTo(scf)
        for i = 1, 35 do
            car:PivotTo(scf:Lerp(tcf, (i / 35) ^ 2 * (3 - 2 * (i / 35))))
            task.wait(0.015)
        end
        car:PivotTo(tcf)
        task.wait(0.15)
        local st, dur, hit = tick(), 10, false
        while tick() - st < dur and car and car.Parent do
            if tp and tp.Character then
                local cth = tp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local ch = tp.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if cth then
                    local ccf = car:GetPivot()
                    local cr = workspace:Raycast(cth.Position + Vector3.new(0,20,0), Vector3.new(0,-40,0), rp)
                    local cgy = cr and cr.Position.Y or cth.Position.Y
                    local tpos = Vector3.new(cth.Position.X, cgy + bo, cth.Position.Z)
                    local d = tpos - ccf.Position
                    if d.Magnitude > 2 then car:PivotTo(CFrame.new(ccf.Position + d.Unit * 3.75, tpos)) end
                    if (ccf.Position - cth.Position).Magnitude <= 7 and not hit then
                        hit = true
                        if ch then ch.Health = 0 end
                        Instance.new("Explosion", workspace).Position = cth.Position
                    end
                end
            else break end
            task.wait(0.03)
        end
        task.wait(0.4)
        if car then car:Destroy() end
    end)
end

local function toggleAura()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    if auraTrack then auraTrack:Stop() auraTrack = nil notify("Aura", "Off")
    else
        auraTrack = safePlayAnimation(char, AURA_ANIM_ID)
        if auraTrack then auraTrack.Looped = true notify("Aura", "On") end
    end
end

local function giveGojo()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not char or not hrp then return end
    local t = Instance.new("Tool")
    t.Name, t.RequiresHandle = "Vazio Roxo", false
    t.Parent = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack") or char
    t.Activated:Connect(function()
        safePlayAnimation(char, CUSTOM_ANIM_ID)
        task.spawn(function()
            local p = fetchAssetAsModel("14819645681")
            if p and p.PrimaryPart then
                pcall(function() p:ScaleTo(300) end)
                for _, x in ipairs(p:GetDescendants()) do
                    if x:IsA("BasePart") then x.Anchored, x.CanCollide = true, false end
                end
                p:PivotTo(hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 2, -20))
                p.Parent = workspace
                local dir = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
                local cn
                cn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
                    if p and p.Parent then p:PivotTo(p:GetPivot() + dir * 150 * dt)
                    else cn:Disconnect() end
                end)
                task.delay(4, function() if cn then cn:Disconnect() end if p then p:Destroy() end end)
            end
        end)
    end)
    notify("Gojo", "Ativado!")
end

local function triggerCommand(action, target, extraArg)
    if not target or target == "" then return end
    if action == "GOJO_DOMAIN" then
        GOJO.cast(target)
        return
    elseif BROADCAST_ACTIONS[action] then
        task.spawn(function() pcall(runBroadcast, action, LocalPlayer.Name, target) end)
    elseif target == LocalPlayer.Name or target == "ALL" then
        executeActionLocally(action, LocalPlayer.Name, extraArg)
    end
    local cmd = ";" .. action .. " " .. target
    if extraArg then cmd = cmd .. " " .. extraArg end
    sendNetCmd(cmd)
    notify("ADM", action .. " → " .. target)
end

local function getPlayerNames()
    local n = {}
    for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do table.insert(n, p.Name) end
    return n
end

local ok, WindUI = pcall(function()
    return loadstring(game:HttpGet("https://github.com/Footagesus/WindUI/releases/latest/download/main.lua"))()
end)
if not ok or not WindUI then
    notify("Erro", "Falha ao carregar WindUI.")
else
    local HUB_NAME   = "Fantasma1l hub"
    local HUB_VER    = "FANTASMA1L HUB | Administrator Version | V1.21"
    local HUB_AUTHOR = "By Fantasma1l / renegado6661l"

    -- chama método do WindUI tentando as duas assinaturas (obj:método e obj.método)
    local function try(obj, name, ...)
        local f = obj and obj[name]
        if type(f) ~= "function" then return false end
        local args = table.pack(...)
        if pcall(f, obj, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n)) then return true end
        return (pcall(f, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n)))
    end

    -- ================== TEMAS EM DEGRADÊ (com sombra) ==================
    local BLACK = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
    local function addTheme(id, c1, c2, accent, light)
        local bg = c1
        pcall(function()
            if WindUI.Gradient then
                bg = WindUI:Gradient({
                    ["0"]   = { Color = c1, Transparency = 0 },
                    ["100"] = { Color = c2, Transparency = 0 },
                }, { Rotation = 35 })
            end
        end)
        local text = light and Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 24) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        pcall(function()
            WindUI:AddTheme({
                Name        = id,
                Accent      = accent,
                Dialog      = c2:Lerp(BLACK, 0.3),
                Outline     = accent,
                Text        = text,
                Placeholder = text:Lerp(c2, 0.5),
                Background  = bg,
                Button      = c1:Lerp(BLACK, 0.2),
                Icon        = accent,
            })
        end)
    end

    local function shaded(base) return base:Lerp(BLACK, 0.3), base:Lerp(BLACK, 0.8) end

    local THEME_LIST = {
        { "Padrão (atual)", "Dark" },
    }
    local function solid(label, base)
        local c1, c2 = shaded(base)
        addTheme("F1L_" .. label, c1, c2, base)
        table.insert(THEME_LIST, { label, "F1L_" .. label })
    end
    solid("Azul",     Color3.fromRGB(40, 110, 255))
    solid("Vermelho", Color3.fromRGB(230, 30, 40))
    solid("Laranja",  Color3.fromRGB(255, 130, 20))
    solid("Roxo",     Color3.fromRGB(150, 60, 230))
    solid("Amarelo",  Color3.fromRGB(250, 200, 20))
    solid("Verde",    Color3.fromRGB(40, 200, 90))
    solid("Cinza",    Color3.fromRGB(130, 135, 145))
    addTheme("F1L_Branco", Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 248), Color3.fromRGB(170, 175, 185), Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 70), true)
    table.insert(THEME_LIST, { "Branco", "F1L_Branco" })
    addTheme("F1L_Preto", Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 26), Color3.fromRGB(4, 4, 6), Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 205))
    table.insert(THEME_LIST, { "Preto", "F1L_Preto" })
    addTheme("F1L_BrancoAzul", Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 250), Color3.fromRGB(50, 110, 240), Color3.fromRGB(40, 90, 220), true)
    table.insert(THEME_LIST, { "Branco e Azul", "F1L_BrancoAzul" })
    addTheme("F1L_LilasVerde", Color3.fromRGB(176, 132, 235), Color3.fromRGB(40, 170, 110), Color3.fromRGB(130, 80, 220))
    table.insert(THEME_LIST, { "Lilás e Verde", "F1L_LilasVerde" })
    addTheme("F1L_PretoLilas", Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 14), Color3.fromRGB(130, 80, 210), Color3.fromRGB(160, 110, 240))
    table.insert(THEME_LIST, { "Preto e Lilás", "F1L_PretoLilas" })

    local THEME_NAMES, THEME_IDS = {}, {}
    for _, t in ipairs(THEME_LIST) do
        table.insert(THEME_NAMES, t[1])
        THEME_IDS[t[1]] = t[2]
    end

    -- ================== JANELA ==================
    local Window = WindUI:CreateWindow({
        Title        = HUB_NAME .. " | " .. HUB_VER,
        Author       = HUB_AUTHOR,
        Icon         = "landmark",
        Theme        = "Dark",
        Transparent  = true,
        Size         = UDim2.fromOffset(560, 300),
        SideBarWidth = 150,
        HasOutline   = false,
        Resizable    = true,
        ScrollBarEnabled = true,
    })

    -- Botão flutuante minimizado: pílula com borda azul → rosa
    pcall(function()
        Window:EditOpenButton({
            Title           = HUB_NAME .. " | Administrador",
            Icon            = "landmark",
            CornerRadius    = UDim.new(1, 0),
            StrokeThickness = 3,
            Color           = ColorSequence.new(
                Color3.fromHex("3B82F6"),
                Color3.fromHex("FF2DAA")
            ),
            OnlyMobile      = false,
            Enabled         = true,
            Draggable       = true,
        })
    end)

    -- ================== AÇÕES ==================
    local function toggleFX(kind)
        local stateTable = (kind == "FLASH") and FXNET.flash or FXNET.aura
        local isOn = stateTable[LocalPlayer.Name] ~= nil
        FXNET.send(kind .. (isOn and "_OFF" or "_ON"), LocalPlayer.Name)
    end

    local function sair()
        pcall(POOL.clear, true)
        pcall(roubeUmBrainrot, "OFF")
        pcall(TORNADO.clear, true)
        if workspace:FindFirstChild("Backrooms_Level0") then pcall(clearBackrooms) end
        task.wait(0.3)
        local chr = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = chr and chr:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local best, bestD
        for _, d in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("SpawnLocation") then
                local dist = hrp and (d.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude or 0
                if not best or dist < bestD then best, bestD = d, dist end
            end
        end
        if hrp and best then
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.CFrame = best.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
        end
        notify("ADM", best and "De volta ao spawn." or "Sem SpawnLocation no mapa; use reset.")
    end

    -- ================== COMANDOS (sem Locust / Xester / Memes) ==================
    local function cmd(title, desc, cb)
        return { kind = "button", title = title, desc = desc, cb = cb }
    end
    local function exec(name, action, withExtra)
        return cmd(";" .. name, "Execute ;" .. name .. " no Jogador", function()
            triggerCommand(action, selectedPlayerName, withExtra and selectedPlayerName or nil)
        end)
    end

    -- ================== CONSTRUÇÃO DAS ABAS (leve: sem aba duplicada) ==================
    local dropdowns = {}
    local built = 0

    local function addTarget(tab)
        local dd = tab:Dropdown({
            Title    = "Selecionar Alvo",
            Values   = getPlayerNames(),
            Value    = selectedPlayerName,
            Callback = function(v) selectedPlayerName = v end,
        })
        table.insert(dropdowns, dd)
    end

    local function addItem(tab, item)
        if item.kind == "input" then
            tab:Input({ Title = item.title, Placeholder = item.placeholder, Callback = item.cb })
        else
            tab:Button({ Title = item.title, Desc = item.desc, Icon = "mouse-pointer-click", Callback = item.cb })
        end
        built += 1
        if built % 8 == 0 then task.wait() end   -- não congela a tela ao montar o painel
    end

    for _, cat in ipairs(CATEGORIES) do
        local tab = Window:Tab({ Title = cat.name, Icon = cat.icon })
        if cat.name ~= "Authorized" then addTarget(tab) end
        tab:Section({ Title = cat.section, Icon = cat.sectionIcon })
        for _, item in ipairs(cat.items) do addItem(tab, item) end
    end

    -- ================== ABA CONFIGURAÇÃO ==================
    local TabCfg = Window:Tab({ Title = "Configuração", Icon = "settings" })

    -- ---- Aparência: transparência do painel ----
    TabCfg:Section({ Title = "Aparência", Icon = "paintbrush" })
    local transparentOn, transparentVal = true, 0.2
    local function applyTransparency()
        pcall(function() WindUI.TransparencyValue = transparentVal end)
        try(Window, "ToggleTransparency", transparentOn)
    end
    TabCfg:Toggle({
        Title = "Painel transparente",
        Desc  = "Liga/desliga a transparência do painel",
        Value = true,
        Callback = function(v) transparentOn = v applyTransparency() end,
    })
    TabCfg:Slider({
        Title = "Transparência do painel",
        Desc  = "0 = sólido | 0.9 = bem transparente",
        Step  = 0.05,
        Value = { Min = 0, Max = 0.9, Default = 0.2 },
        Callback = function(v)
            transparentVal = tonumber(v) or 0.2
            applyTransparency()
        end,
    })

    -- ---- Cor do painel (degradê com sombra) ----
    TabCfg:Section({ Title = "Cor do painel", Icon = "palette" })
    TabCfg:Dropdown({
        Title  = "Tema / cor",
        Desc   = "Azul, vermelho, laranja, roxo, amarelo, verde, cinza, branco, preto e combinações",
        Values = THEME_NAMES,
        Value  = "Padrão (atual)",
        Callback = function(name)
            local id = THEME_IDS[name]
            if id then pcall(function() WindUI:SetTheme(id) end) end
        end,
    })

    -- ---- Fundo (imagem) ----
    TabCfg:Section({ Title = "Fundo (imagem)", Icon = "image" })
    local BG_IDS = {
        ["Gojo"] = 99188277568430,
        ["Toji"] = 99521340506230,
        ["Halloween"] = 72671289611166,
        ["Dragon"] = 123754864736277,
        ["Ilustração"] = 121502907054918,
    }
    local bgTransparency = 0.6
    local bgLabel

    -- acha o quadro principal do painel (o "corpo" da janela do WindUI)
    local function findMainFrame()
        local main
        pcall(function() main = Window.UIElements.Main end)
        if typeof(main) == "Instance" then return main end

        local roots = {}
        pcall(function() table.insert(roots, gethui()) end)
        pcall(function() table.insert(roots, game:GetService("CoreGui")) end)
        table.insert(roots, LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui"))
        local best, bestArea = nil, 0
        for _, r in ipairs(roots) do
            local okc, kids = pcall(function() return r:GetChildren() end)
            if okc then
                for _, g in ipairs(kids) do
                    if g:IsA("ScreenGui") and string.find(string.lower(g.Name), "windui") then
                        for _, d in ipairs(g:GetDescendants()) do
                            if d:IsA("Frame") and d.Name == "Main" then return d end
                        end
                        for _, d in ipairs(g:GetDescendants()) do
                            if d:IsA("Frame") and d:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner") then
                                local area = d.AbsoluteSize.X * d.AbsoluteSize.Y
                                if d.AbsoluteSize.X < g.AbsoluteSize.X * 0.98 and area > bestArea then
                                    best, bestArea = d, area
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return best
    end

    local function setBackground(name)
        if bgLabel then
            pcall(function() bgLabel:Destroy() end)
            bgLabel = nil
        end
        local id = BG_IDS[name]
        if not id then return end
        local host = findMainFrame()
        if not host then
            notify("Fundo", "Não achei o painel pra colocar a imagem.")
            return
        end
        local src = resolveImage(id)   -- converte Decal -> imagem real
        local lbl = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        lbl.Name = "F1L_Background"
        lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lbl.Image = src
        lbl.ImageTransparency = bgTransparency
        lbl.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
        lbl.ZIndex = 50            -- por cima do fundo do painel, pra sempre aparecer
        lbl.Active = false
        pcall(function() lbl.Interactable = false end)
        local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 16)
        corner.Parent = lbl
        lbl.Parent = host
        bgLabel = lbl
        -- plano B: se a imagem não carregar em 4s, usa a miniatura do asset
        task.delay(4, function()
            if bgLabel == lbl and lbl.Parent and not lbl.IsLoaded then
                lbl.Image = "rbxthumb://type=Asset&id=" .. tostring(id) .. "&w=420&h=420"
            end
        end)
    end

    TabCfg:Dropdown({
        Title  = "Imagem de fundo",
        Desc   = "Gojo, Toji, Halloween, Dragon ou Ilustração",
        Values = { "Nenhum", "Gojo", "Toji", "Halloween", "Dragon", "Ilustração" },
        Value  = "Nenhum",
        Callback = function(name)
            task.spawn(function() pcall(setBackground, name) end)
        end,
    })
    TabCfg:Slider({
        Title = "Transparência da imagem",
        Desc  = "0 = imagem forte | 1 = invisível",
        Step  = 0.05,
        Value = { Min = 0, Max = 1, Default = 0.6 },
        Callback = function(v)
            bgTransparency = tonumber(v) or 0.6
            if bgLabel and bgLabel.Parent then bgLabel.ImageTransparency = bgTransparency end
        end,
    })

    pcall(function() Window:SelectTab(1) end)

    -- Mantém o dropdown de alvo atualizado
    local function refreshPlayers()
        local names = getPlayerNames()
        for _, dd in ipairs(dropdowns) do
            pcall(function()
                if dd.Refresh then dd:Refresh(names)
                elseif dd.SetValues then dd:SetValues(names) end
            end)
        end
    end
    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(refreshPlayers)
    Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function() task.defer(refreshPlayers) end)
end

end -- fim IS_ADMIN

local Players            = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage  = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local RunService         = game:GetService("RunService")
local StarterGui         = game:GetService("StarterGui")
local Lighting           = game:GetService("Lighting")
local LocalPlayer        = Players.LocalPlayer

local ICON_ID = "rbxassetid://72455921762458"

local function notify(t, txt, dur)
    pcall(function()
        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", { Title = t, Text = txt, Duration = dur or 5 })
    end)
end

local function log(txt)
    print("[Fantasma1l] " .. txt)
    pcall(function() rconsoleprint("[Fantasma1l] " .. txt .. "\n") end)
end

notify("Fantasma1l Hub", "A carregar V8.5.6 com Server Lag...", 2)

local function getHRP()
    local c = LocalPlayer.Character
    return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

-- ================== NETWORK BYPASS ==================
local function applyNetworkBypass()
    pcall(function()
        if sethiddenproperty then
            sethiddenproperty(LocalPlayer, "MaximumSimulationRadius", math.huge)
            sethiddenproperty(LocalPlayer, "SimulationRadius", math.huge)
        end
        if setsimulationradius then
            setsimulationradius(math.huge, math.huge)
        end
    end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.1) do
        applyNetworkBypass()
    end
end)

-- ================== CONFIG VOID & STATE ==================
local VOID_Y      = -500
local FALL_SPEED  = 8000

-- ================== PHONE / LAG SYSTEM ==================
local isLaggingServer = false

local function getPhoneTool()
    local backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not backpack or not character then return nil end

    local function findPhone(container)
        if not container then return nil end
        for _, item in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            if item:IsA("Tool") and (item.Name:lower():find("phone") or item.Name:lower():find("celular")) then
                return item
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local item = findPhone(character) or findPhone(backpack)
    if item then return item end

    pcall(function()
        local re = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("RE")
        if re and re:FindFirstChild("1Tool") then
            re["1Tool"]:InvokeServer("PickingTools", "Phone")
            re["1Tool"]:InvokeServer("PickingTools", "CellPhone")
        end
    end)

    local t0 = tick()
    repeat
        task.wait(0.05)
        item = findPhone(backpack) or findPhone(character)
    until item or tick() - t0 > 2

    return item
end

local function startServerLag()
    task.spawn(function()
        while isLaggingServer do
            pcall(function()
                local phone = getPhoneTool()
                local character = LocalPlayer.Character
                local hum = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local re = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("RE")

                if re and re:FindFirstChild("1Tool") then
                    -- Puxa e duplica chamadas de ferramentas Phone no servidor
                    for i = 1, 10 do
                        re["1Tool"]:InvokeServer("PickingTools", "Phone")
                    end
                end

                if phone and hum then
                    -- Alterna o item para gerar desincronização em massa no servidor
                    hum:EquipTool(phone)
                    task.wait(0.01)
                    phone.Parent = LocalPlayer.Backpack
                end
            end)
            task.wait(0.01)
        end
    end)
end

-- ================== COUCH AUTOMÁTICO v2 ==================
local function getCouch()
    local backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not backpack or not character then return nil end

    local function findCouch(container)
        if not container then return nil end
        for _, item in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            if item:IsA("Tool") and (item.Name:lower():find("couch") or item.Name:lower():find("sofa")) then
                return item
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local item = findCouch(character) or findCouch(backpack)
    if item then return item end

    local function tryPull()
        pcall(function()
            for _, gui in ipairs(LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:GetDescendants()) do
                if gui:IsA("GuiButton") then
                    local texto = (gui.Name .. " " .. (gui:IsA("TextButton") and gui.Text or "")):lower()
                    if texto:find("couch") or texto:find("sofa") then
                        pcall(function() gui.MouseButton1Click:Fire() end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            local re = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("RE")
            if not re then return end
            local nomes = { "1Tool", "1Too1l", "1Tools", "Tool", "1GiveTool", "GiveTool", "1Tool1" }
            local argSets = {
                { "PickingTools", "Couch" }, { "GiveTool", "Couch" }, { "Couch" },
                { "PickingTools", "Sofa" }, { "GiveTool", "Sofa" },
            }
            for _, rn in ipairs(nomes) do
                local remote = re:FindFirstChild(rn)
                if remote then
                    for _, args in ipairs(argSets) do
                        pcall(function() remote:FireServer(unpack(args)) end)
                        pcall(function() remote:InvokeServer(unpack(args)) end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    local t0 = tick()
    while tick() - t0 < 5 do
        tryPull()
        local tw = tick()
        repeat
            task.wait(0.05)
            item = findCouch(backpack) or findCouch(character)
        until item or tick() - tw > 1
        if item then break end
        task.wait(0.2)
    end

    if item then
        local hum = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if item.Parent == backpack and hum then
            pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(item) end)
        end
    end
    return item
end

-- ================== SOCCERBALL AUTOMÁTICO ==================
local function getSoccerBall()
    local backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not backpack or not character then return nil end

    local function findBall(container)
        if not container then return nil end
        for _, item in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            if item:IsA("Tool") and item.Name:lower():find("soccerball") then
                return item
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local item = findBall(character) or findBall(backpack)
    if item then return item end

    pcall(function()
        local re = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("RE")
        if re and re:FindFirstChild("1Tool") then
            re["1Tool"]:InvokeServer("PickingTools", "SoccerBall")
        end
    end)

    local t0 = tick()
    repeat
        task.wait(0.05)
        item = findBall(backpack) or findBall(character)
    until item or tick() - t0 > 2

    if item then
        local hum = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if item.Parent == backpack and hum then
            pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(item) end)
        end
    end
    return item
end

-- ================== SISTEMA DE PROPS ==================
local function getPropPart(prop)
    if not prop then return nil end
    if prop:IsA("BasePart") then return prop end
    return prop.PrimaryPart or prop:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
end

local function isBadProp(obj)
    local cur = obj.Parent
    while cur and cur ~= workspace do
        if cur:IsA("Model") and (cur:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or cur.Name:find("Car") or cur.Name:find("Bus")) then
            return true
        end
        if cur:IsA("Tool") or cur.Name == "Vehicles" then return true end
        cur = cur.Parent
    end
    return false
end

local function scanProps()
    local movables = {}
    for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if (obj:IsA("Model") or obj:IsA("BasePart")) and not isBadProp(obj) then
            if obj:FindFirstChild("SetCurrentCFrame") then
                table.insert(movables, obj)
            end
        end
    end
    if #movables == 0 then
        for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if (obj:IsA("Model") or obj:IsA("BasePart")) and not isBadProp(obj) then
                if obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Seat", true) or obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("VehicleSeat", true) then
                    table.insert(movables, obj)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return movables
end

local function getNearestProp()
    local props = scanProps()
    if #props == 0 then return nil end
    local hrp = getHRP()
    local best, bestD = props[1], math.huge
    for _, p in ipairs(props) do
        local root = getPropPart(p)
        if root and hrp then
            local d = (root.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
            if d < bestD then bestD = d; best = p end
        end
    end
    return best
end

-- ================== MOVE PROP ==================
local function moveProp(prop, cf)
    if not prop or not prop.Parent then return end
    local remote = prop:FindFirstChild("SetCurrentCFrame")

    if remote and remote:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
        pcall(function() remote:InvokeServer(cf) end)
    elseif remote and remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
        pcall(function() remote:FireServer(cf) end)
    end

    pcall(function()
        if prop:IsA("Model") then
            if prop.PrimaryPart then prop:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(cf)
            else prop:PivotTo(cf) end
        else
            prop.CFrame = cf
        end
    end)
end

-- ================== SPAWN DO BUS ==================
local function GetBus()
    local v = workspace:FindFirstChild("Vehicles")
    return v and (v:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "Car") or v:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "Bus"))
end

local function SpawnBus()
    local bus = GetBus()
    if bus then return bus end
    local hrp = getHRP()
    if not hrp then return nil end
    local orig = hrp.CFrame
    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(1118.81, 75.998, -1138.61)
    task.wait(0.5)
    local re = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("RE")
    if re and re:FindFirstChild("1Cair") then
        pcall(function() re["1Cair"]:FireServer("PickingCar", "SchoolBus") end)
        task.wait(0.3)
        pcall(function() re["1Cair"]:FireServer("PickingCar", "Bus") end)
    end
    local t0 = tick()
    repeat
        bus = GetBus()
        task.wait(0.3)
    until bus or tick() - t0 > 5
    hrp.CFrame = orig
    return bus
end

-- ================== BUS COM MOTORISTA ==================
local function doBusDriverKill(targetPlayer)
    if not targetPlayer or targetPlayer == LocalPlayer then return end
    applyNetworkBypass()
    local tc = targetPlayer.Character
    local tr = tc and tc:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local thum = tc and tc:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not tr or not thum then return end
    local myChr = LocalPlayer.Character
    local myHrp = getHRP()
    local myHum = myChr and myChr:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not myHrp or not myHum then return end
    local originalCFrame = myHrp.CFrame

    local bus = SpawnBus()
    if not bus then return end
    local driverSeat = bus:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("VehicleSeat", true)
    if not driverSeat then driverSeat = bus:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Seat", true) or bus.PrimaryPart end
    if not driverSeat then return end

    myHrp.CFrame = driverSeat.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 2, 0)
    task.wait(0.2)
    pcall(function() driverSeat:Sit(myHum) end)
    task.wait(0.4)

    local angle, startTime = 0, tick()
    while tick() - startTime < 4.5 do
        if not tr or not tr.Parent or thum.Health <= 0 then break end
        applyNetworkBypass()
        angle = angle + 45
        pcall(function()
            bus:PivotTo(tr.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(angle), 0))
            driverSeat.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -10, 0)
        end)
        if thum.Sit then break end
        task.wait()
    end

    local dropStart = tick()
    while tick() - dropStart < 3.5 do
        if not tr or not tr.Parent or thum.Health <= 0 then break end
        applyNetworkBypass()
        pcall(function()
            driverSeat.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -FALL_SPEED, 0)
            tr.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -FALL_SPEED, 0)
            bus:PivotTo(CFrame.new(tr.Position))
        end)
        task.wait()
        if tr.Position.Y <= VOID_Y then break end
    end

    pcall(function() myHum.Sit = false end)
    task.wait(0.1)
    if myHrp and myHrp.Parent then
        myHrp.CFrame = originalCFrame
        myHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        myHrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end
    pcall(function() thum.Health = 0; tr:BreakJoints() end)
    pcall(function()
        if ReplicatedStorage.RE and ReplicatedStorage.RE:FindFirstChild("1Cair") then
            ReplicatedStorage.RE["1Cair"]:FireServer("DeleteAllVehicles")
        end
    end)
end

-- ================== FLING BALL ==================
local function executeFlingBall(targetPlayer)
    if not targetPlayer or targetPlayer == LocalPlayer then return end
    applyNetworkBypass()
    local tc = targetPlayer.Character
    local tr = tc and tc:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local thum = tc and tc:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not tr or not thum then return end

    local ballTool = getSoccerBall()
    if not ballTool then return end
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local myHum = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if myHum then pcall(function() myHum:EquipTool(ballTool) end) end
    task.wait(0.2)

    local handle = ballTool:FindFirstChild("Handle") or ballTool:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
    if not handle then return end
    ballTool.Parent = workspace
    handle.CanCollide = false

    local bv = handle:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyVelocity") or Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
    bv.Name = "FlingVelocity"
    bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(9e8, 9e8, 9e8)
    bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
    bv.P = 9e9
    bv.Parent = handle

    local startTime = tick()
    while tick() - startTime < 3 and tr and tr.Parent and thum.Health > 0 do
        applyNetworkBypass()
        pcall(function()
            handle.CFrame = tr.CFrame
            handle.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(math.random(-9e8, 9e8), 9e8, math.random(-9e8, 9e8))
            tr.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 9e8, 0)
        end)
        task.wait()
    end
    pcall(function() ballTool:Destroy() end)
end

-- ================== PROPMAKER KILL ==================
local function executePropMakerKill(targetPlayer)
    if not targetPlayer or targetPlayer == LocalPlayer then return end
    applyNetworkBypass()

    local tc = targetPlayer.Character
    local tr = tc and tc:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local thum = tc and tc:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not tr or not thum then return end

    local myHrp = getHRP()
    if not myHrp then return end
    local originalCFrame = myHrp.CFrame

    local prop = getNearestProp()
    if not prop then
        notify("PropMaker", "Nenhum prop no mapa! Spawna um objeto pelo menu do jogo.", 8)
        return
    end

    local propRoot = getPropPart(prop)
    if not propRoot then return end

    notify("PropMaker", "A travar " .. targetPlayer.Name .. "...")

    local targetOffset = CFrame.new(0, -0.5, 0)
    pcall(function()
        local modelSize = tc:GetExtentsSize()
        if modelSize and modelSize.Y then
            local halfHeight = modelSize.Y / 2
            targetOffset = CFrame.new(0, -halfHeight, 0)
        end
    end)

    for _, part in ipairs(prop:GetDescendants()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") then
            part.CanCollide = true
        end
    end

    myHrp.CFrame = tr.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3)
    task.wait(0.05)

    local snapStart = tick()
    while tick() - snapStart < 0.3 do
        applyNetworkBypass()
        pcall(function()
            if propRoot and propRoot.Parent then
                propRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(2000, 2000, 2000)
                propRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -500, 0)
            end
            tr.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -500, 0)
            moveProp(prop, tr.CFrame * targetOffset)
        end)
        task.wait(0.02)
    end

    myHrp.CFrame = originalCFrame
    myHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero

    notify("PropMaker", "Puxando para o Void...")
    local dropStart = tick()
    local curTargetCF = tr.CFrame

    while tick() - dropStart < 3.0 do
        if not tr or not tr.Parent or thum.Health <= 0 then break end
        applyNetworkBypass()

        pcall(function()
            curTargetCF = curTargetCF * CFrame.new(0, -80, 0)
            tr.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -FALL_SPEED, 0)
            if propRoot and propRoot.Parent then
                propRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -FALL_SPEED, 0)
                propRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(500, 500, 500)
            end
            moveProp(prop, curTargetCF * targetOffset)
        end)
        task.wait(0.02)
    end

    if propRoot and propRoot.Parent then
        local propDropStart = tick()
        while tick() - propDropStart < 1.0 and propRoot and propRoot.Parent do
            applyNetworkBypass()
            pcall(function()
                propRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -FALL_SPEED, 0)
                local currentCF = propRoot.CFrame
                moveProp(prop, currentCF * CFrame.new(0, -400, 0))
            end)
            task.wait()
        end
    end

    pcall(function() thum.Health = 0; tr:BreakJoints() end)
end

-- ================== REDZ LIBRARY ==================
task.spawn(function()
    local redz = nil
    local urls = {
        "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/minhdepzai-v/LibraryRobloc/refs/heads/main/RedzLibrary.lua",
        "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/REDzHUB/RedzLibV5/main/Source.Lua"
    }
    for _, url in ipairs(urls) do
        local ok, lib = pcall(function() return loadstring(game:HttpGet(url))() end)
        if ok and lib then redz = lib break end
    end
    if not redz then
        notify("Erro Critico", "Falha ao carregar a biblioteca UI!")
        return
    end

    local Window = redz:MakeWindow({
        Title = "Fantasma1l Hub",
        SubTitle = "Brookhaven Edition v8.5.6",
        SaveFolder = "Fantasma1l_Brookhaven"
    })
    Window:AddMinimizeButton({
        Button = { Image = ICON_ID, BackgroundTransparency = 0 },
        Corner = { CornerRadius = UDim.new(35, 1) },
    })

    local TabHome      = Window:MakeTab({ "Inicio", "home" })
    local TabTroll     = Window:MakeTab({ "Troll e Acoes", "user" })
    TabTroll:AddSection({ "Troll e Acoes" })
    local TabServerLag = Window:MakeTab({ "Server Lag", "wifi" })
    local TabProt      = Window:MakeTab({ "Protecao", "shield" })
    local TabAudio     = Window:MakeTab({ "Audio", "music" })
    Window:SelectTab(TabHome)

    TabHome:AddSection({ "Informacoes" })
    TabHome:AddParagraph({ "Bem-vindo", "Fantasma1l Hub - Brookhaven v8.5.6" })
    TabHome:AddButton({ "Rejoin Server", function()
        game:GetService("TeleportService"):TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, LocalPlayer)
    end })

    -- TAB SERVER LAG
    TabServerLag:AddSection({ "Controlo de Lag do Servidor" })
    TabServerLag:AddToggle({
        Name = "Lag (Duplicar Phone)",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            isLaggingServer = v
            if v then
                notify("Server Lag", "A iniciar lag com Phone no servidor...")
                startServerLag()
            else
                notify("Server Lag", "Lag do servidor desativado.")
            end
        end
    })

    local selectedPlayerNameBH = nil
    getgenv().Target = nil

    local function listPlayers()
        local names = {}
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= LocalPlayer then table.insert(names, p.Name) end
        end
        if #names == 0 then table.insert(names, "Ninguem") end
        return names
    end

    local dd = TabTroll:AddDropdown({
        Name = "Selecionar Jogador",
        Options = listPlayers(),
        Default = "",
        Callback = function(v) selectedPlayerNameBH = v getgenv().Target = v end
    })
    TabTroll:AddButton({ "Atualizar Lista", function() dd:Set(listPlayers()) end })

    local specConn
    TabTroll:AddToggle({ Name = "View Player (Spectar)", Default = false, Callback = function(v)
        if specConn then specConn:Disconnect() specConn = nil end
        if v and selectedPlayerNameBH then
            specConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                local tp = Players:FindFirstChild(selectedPlayerNameBH)
                if tp and tp.Character then
                    local h = tp.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if h then workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = h end
                end
            end)
        else
            local chr = LocalPlayer.Character
            local h = chr and chr:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if h then workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = h end
        end
    end })

    TabTroll:AddSection({ "Metodos" })
    local methodKill = "Prop"
    TabTroll:AddDropdown({
        Name = "Metodo",
        Options = { "Prop", "Couch", "Bus", "Fling" },
        Default = "Prop",
        Callback = function(v) methodKill = v end
    })

    local function executeMethodOnPlayer(tp, method)
        if not tp or tp == LocalPlayer then return end
        applyNetworkBypass()
        local tr = tp.Character and tp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local thum = tp.Character and tp.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not tr or not thum then return end
        local myChr = LocalPlayer.Character
        local myHrp = getHRP()
        local myHum = myChr and myChr:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not myHrp or not myHum then return end
        local originalCFrame = myHrp.CFrame

        if method == "Couch" then
            local item = getCouch()
            if item then
                pcall(function() myHum:EquipTool(item) end)
                task.wait(0.3)
                local handle = item:FindFirstChild("Handle") or item:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                if handle then
                    myHrp.CFrame = tr.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -1, 0)
                    task.wait(0.1)
                    item.Parent = workspace
                    local SAFE_Y, RETURN_TIME, saved, startTime = -60, 1.0, false, tick()
                    local function safeReturn()
                        if saved then return end
                        saved = true
                        pcall(function() myHum:UnequipTools() end)
                        pcall(function() myHum.Sit = false end)
                        if myHrp and myHrp.Parent then
                            myHrp.CFrame = originalCFrame
                            myHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            myHrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        end
                    end
                    local dropLoop
                    dropLoop = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                        if not tr or not tr.Parent or thum.Health <= 0 or tick() - startTime > 3.5 then
                            if dropLoop then dropLoop:Disconnect() end
                            safeReturn()
                            pcall(function() thum.Health = 0; tr:BreakJoints() end)
                            if item and item.Parent then pcall(function() item:Destroy() end) end
                            return
                        end
                        applyNetworkBypass()
                        pcall(function()
                            tr.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -FALL_SPEED, 0)
                            handle.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -FALL_SPEED, 0)
                            handle.CFrame = tr.CFrame
                        end)
                        if not saved and (tr.Position.Y <= SAFE_Y or tick() - startTime >= RETURN_TIME) then
                            safeReturn()
                        end
                    end)
                    task.wait(3.8)
                end
            else
                notify("Erro", "Nao foi possivel pegar o Couch.")
            end
        elseif method == "Prop" then
            executePropMakerKill(tp)
        elseif method == "Bus" then
            doBusDriverKill(tp)
        elseif method == "Fling" then
            executeFlingBall(tp)
        end

        if myHrp and myHrp.Parent then
            myHrp.CFrame = originalCFrame
            myHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
    end

    TabTroll:AddSection({ "Acoes de Kill" })

    TabTroll:AddButton({ "KILL (Alvo Selecionado)", function()
        local targetName = getgenv().Target or selectedPlayerNameBH
        if not targetName or targetName == "Ninguem" then notify("Erro", "Selecione um jogador.") return end
        local tp = Players:FindFirstChild(targetName)
        if tp then
            notify("Kill", "A executar em " .. tp.Name .. " [" .. methodKill .. "]")
            task.spawn(function() executeMethodOnPlayer(tp, methodKill) end)
        else
            notify("Erro", "Jogador nao encontrado.")
        end
    end })

    TabTroll:AddButton({ "KILL ALL (Todos no Servidor)", function()
        notify("Kill All", "A iniciar Kill All com " .. methodKill .. "!")
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= LocalPlayer then
                task.spawn(function() executeMethodOnPlayer(p, methodKill) end)
                task.wait(0.2)
            end
        end
    end })

    TabTroll:AddButton({ "KILL PROP ALL (Move Prop ate o Alvo)", function()
        notify("Kill Prop All", "Props a enviar ate aos alvos!")
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= LocalPlayer then
                task.spawn(function() executePropMakerKill(p) end)
                task.wait(0.3)
            end
        end
    end })

    -- Proteção & Anti-Lag
    local PROT = { antiSit = false, antiFling = false, antiLag = false, conns = {} }

    TabProt:AddSection({ "Anti-Lag do Cliente" })
    TabProt:AddToggle({ Name = "Ativar Anti-Lag", Default = false, Callback = function(v)
        PROT.antiLag = v
        if v then
            pcall(function()
                settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = Enum.QualityLevel.Level01
                Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
                Lighting.FogEnd = 9e9
                for _, v in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                    if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Trail") or v:IsA("Smoke") or v:IsA("Fire") or v:IsA("Sparkles") then
                        v.Enabled = false
                    end
                end
            end)
            notify("Anti-Lag", "Otimizacoes de Anti-Lag ativadas!")
        else
            pcall(function()
                settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = Enum.QualityLevel.Automatic
                Lighting.GlobalShadows = true
            end)
            notify("Anti-Lag", "Otimizacoes de Anti-Lag desativadas.")
        end
    end })

    TabProt:AddSection({ "Anti-Sit" })
    TabProt:AddToggle({ Name = "Ativar Anti-Sit", Default = false, Callback = function(v)
        PROT.antiSit = v
        if v then
            local conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                local chr = LocalPlayer.Character
                local hum = chr and chr:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum and hum.Sit then
                    hum.Sit = false
                    hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
                end
            end)
            table.insert(PROT.conns, conn)
        end
    end })

    TabProt:AddSection({ "Anti-Fling" })
    TabProt:AddToggle({ Name = "Ativar Anti-Fling", Default = false, Callback = function(v)
        PROT.antiFling = v
        if v then
            local conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                local hrp = getHRP()
                if not hrp then return end
                local v3 = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                if v3.Magnitude > 500 or v3.Y < -300 or v3.Y > 500 then
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
            end)
            table.insert(PROT.conns, conn)
        end
    end })

    TabProt:AddButton({ "Desativar Toda a Protecao", function()
        for _, conn in ipairs(PROT.conns) do pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
        PROT.conns = {}
        notify("Protecao", "Toda a protecao foi desativada.")
    end })

    -- Áudio
    TabAudio:AddSection({ "Boombox" })
    local customAudioId = ""
    TabAudio:AddTextBox({ Name = "ID da Musica", Default = "", Placeholder = "1848354536",
        Callback = function(v) customAudioId = v end })
    TabAudio:AddButton({ "Tocar no Boombox", function()
        local id = tonumber(customAudioId)
        if not id then notify("Erro", "Coloque um ID valido.") return end
        pcall(function()
            local br = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("RE") and ReplicatedStorage.RE:FindFirstChild("1BoomBox")
            if br then br:InvokeServer(id) else notify("Erro", "Boombox nao encontrado.") end
        end)
    end })

    notify("Fantasma1l Hub", "Brookhaven Edition v8.5.6 carregado!")
end)
